domain rt  {

  unique function intType(): rt

  unique function longType(): rt

  unique function shortType(): rt

  unique function byteType(): rt

  unique function boolType(): rt

  unique function charType(): rt

  unique function unitType(): rt

  unique function nothingType(): rt

  unique function anyType(): rt

  unique function functionType(): rt

  unique function stringType(): rt

  unique function B(): rt

  function nullValue(): Ref

  function unitValue(): Ref

  function isSubtype(t1: rt, t2: rt): Bool

  function typeOf(r: Ref): rt

  function nullable(t: rt): rt

  function intToRef(v: Int): Ref

  function intFromRef(r: Ref): Int

  function longToRef(v: Int): Ref

  function longFromRef(r: Ref): Int

  function shortToRef(v: Int): Ref

  function shortFromRef(r: Ref): Int

  function byteToRef(v: Int): Ref

  function byteFromRef(r: Ref): Int

  function boolToRef(v: Bool): Ref

  function boolFromRef(r: Ref): Bool

  function charFromRef(r: Ref): Int

  function stringToRef(v: Seq[Int]): Ref

  function stringFromRef(r: Ref): Seq[Int]

  axiom subtypeReflexive {
    (forall t: rt ::isSubtype(t, t))
  }

  axiom subtypeTransitive {
    (forall t1: rt, t2: rt, t3: rt ::
      { isSubtype(t1, t2), isSubtype(t2, t3) }
      { isSubtype(t1, t2), isSubtype(t1, t3) }
      { isSubtype(t2, t3), isSubtype(t1, t3) }
      isSubtype(t1, t2) && isSubtype(t2, t3) ==> isSubtype(t1, t3))
  }

  axiom subtypeAntisymmetric {
    (forall t1: rt, t2: rt ::
      { isSubtype(t1, t2), isSubtype(t2, t1) }
      isSubtype(t1, t2) && isSubtype(t2, t1) ==> t1 == t2)
  }

  axiom nullableIdempotent {
    (forall t: rt ::
      { nullable(nullable(t)) }
      nullable(nullable(t)) == nullable(t))
  }

  axiom nullableSupertype {
    (forall t: rt :: { nullable(t) } isSubtype(t, nullable(t)))
  }

  axiom nullablePreservesSubtype {
    (forall t1: rt, t2: rt ::
      { isSubtype(nullable(t1), nullable(t2)) }
      isSubtype(t1, t2) ==> isSubtype(nullable(t1), nullable(t2)))
  }

  axiom nullableAnySupertype {
    (forall t: rt ::isSubtype(t, nullable(anyType())))
  }

  axiom {
    isSubtype(intType(), anyType())
  }

  axiom {
    isSubtype(longType(), anyType())

    isSubtype(B(), anyType())

  axiom supertypeOfNothing {
    (forall t: rt ::isSubtype(nothingType(), t))
  }

  axiom anyNotNullableTypeLevel {
    (forall t: rt ::!isSubtype(nullable(t), anyType()))
  }

  axiom nullSmartcastValueLevel {
    (forall r: Ref, t: rt ::
      { isSubtype(typeOf(r), nullable(t)) }
      isSubtype(typeOf(r), nullable(t)) ==>
      r == nullValue() || isSubtype(typeOf(r), t))
  }

  axiom nothingEmpty {
    (forall r: Ref ::!isSubtype(typeOf(r), nothingType()))
  }

  axiom nullSmartcastTypeLevel {
    (forall t1: rt, t2: rt ::
      { isSubtype(t1, anyType()), isSubtype(t1, nullable(t2)) }
      isSubtype(t1, anyType()) && isSubtype(t1, nullable(t2)) ==>
      isSubtype(t1, t2))
  }

  axiom typeOfNull {
    isSubtype(typeOf(nullValue()), nullable(nothingType()))
  }

  axiom anyNotNullableValueLevel {
    !isSubtype(typeOf(nullValue()), anyType())
  }

  axiom typeOfUnit {
    isSubtype(typeOf(unitValue()), unitType())
  }

  axiom uniquenessOfUnit {
    (forall r: Ref ::
      { isSubtype(typeOf(r), unitType()) }
      isSubtype(typeOf(r), unitType()) ==> r == unitValue())
  }

  axiom {
    (forall r: Ref ::
      { intFromRef(r) }
      isSubtype(typeOf(r), intType()) ==>
      intFromRef(r) >= -2147483648 && intFromRef(r) <= 2147483647)

  axiom {
    (forall v: Int ::
      { isSubtype(typeOf(intToRef(v)), intType()) }

        { stringFromRef(r)[index] }
        index >= 0 && index < |stringFromRef(r)| ==>
        stringFromRef(r)[index] >= 0 && stringFromRef(r)[index] <= 65535))
  }

  axiom {
    (forall v: Seq[Int] ::

      |v| <= 2147483647 &&

        { v[index] }
        index >= 0 && index < |v| ==> v[index] >= 0 && v[index] <= 65535) ==>
      isSubtype(typeOf(stringToRef(v)), stringType()))
  }

function addCharInt(arg_1: Ref, arg_2: Ref): Ref
  requires isSubtype(typeOf(arg_1), charType())
  requires isSubtype(typeOf(arg_2), intType())
  requires charFromRef(arg_1) + intFromRef(arg_2) >= 0 &&
    charFromRef(arg_1) + intFromRef(arg_2) <= 65535
  ensures isSubtype(typeOf(result), charType())
  ensures charFromRef(result) == charFromRef(arg_1) + intFromRef(arg_2)


function addStringChar(arg_1: Ref, arg_2: Ref): Ref
  requires isSubtype(typeOf(arg_1), stringType())
  requires isSubtype(typeOf(arg_2), charType())
  ensures isSubtype(typeOf(result), stringType())
  ensures |stringFromRef(arg_1)| < 2147483647 ==>
    stringFromRef(result) ==
    stringFromRef(arg_1) ++ Seq(charFromRef(arg_2))

    stringFromRef(result) == stringFromRef(arg_1) ++ stringFromRef(arg_2)

  ensures intFromRef(result) ==

predicate B_unique(this: Ref) {
  isSubtype(typeOf(this), B())
}

method con() returns (v_ret_0: Ref)
  ensures isSubtype(typeOf(v_ret_0), B())
{
  v_ret_0 := new()
  inhale typeOf(v_ret_0) == B()
  fold acc(B_unique(v_ret_0), write)
  label lbl_ret_0
  unfold acc(B_unique(v_ret_0), write)
}

  axiom subtypeReflexive {
    (forall t: rt ::isSubtype(t, t))
  }

  axiom subtypeTransitive {
    (forall t1: rt, t2: rt, t3: rt ::
      { isSubtype(t1, t2), isSubtype(t2, t3) }
      { isSubtype(t1, t2), isSubtype(t1, t3) }
      { isSubtype(t2, t3), isSubtype(t1, t3) }
      isSubtype(t1, t2) && isSubtype(t2, t3) ==> isSubtype(t1, t3))
  }

  axiom subtypeAntisymmetric {
    (forall t1: rt, t2: rt ::
      { isSubtype(t1, t2), isSubtype(t2, t1) }
      isSubtype(t1, t2) && isSubtype(t2, t1) ==> t1 == t2)
  }

  axiom nullableIdempotent {
    (forall t: rt ::
      { nullable(nullable(t)) }
      nullable(nullable(t)) == nullable(t))
  }

  axiom nullableSupertype {
    (forall t: rt :: { nullable(t) } isSubtype(t, nullable(t)))
  }

  axiom nullablePreservesSubtype {
    (forall t1: rt, t2: rt ::
      { isSubtype(nullable(t1), nullable(t2)) }
      isSubtype(t1, t2) ==> isSubtype(nullable(t1), nullable(t2)))
  }

  axiom nullableAnySupertype {
    (forall t: rt ::isSubtype(t, nullable(anyType())))
  }

  axiom {
    isSubtype(intType(), anyType())
  }

  axiom {
    isSubtype(longType(), anyType())
  }

  axiom {
    isSubtype(shortType(), anyType())
  }

  axiom {
    isSubtype(byteType(), anyType())
  }

  axiom {
    isSubtype(boolType(), anyType())
  }

  axiom {
    isSubtype(charType(), anyType())
  }

  axiom {
    isSubtype(unitType(), anyType())
  }

  axiom {
    isSubtype(nothingType(), anyType())
  }

  axiom {
    isSubtype(anyType(), anyType())
  }

  axiom {
    isSubtype(functionType(), anyType())
  }

  axiom {
    isSubtype(stringType(), anyType())
  }

  axiom {
    isSubtype(A(), anyType())
  }

  axiom {
    isSubtype(B(), anyType())
  }

  axiom supertypeOfNothing {
    (forall t: rt ::isSubtype(nothingType(), t))
  }

  axiom anyNotNullableTypeLevel {
    (forall t: rt ::!isSubtype(nullable(t), anyType()))
  }

  axiom nullSmartcastValueLevel {
    (forall r: Ref, t: rt ::
      { isSubtype(typeOf(r), nullable(t)) }
      isSubtype(typeOf(r), nullable(t)) ==>
      r == nullValue() || isSubtype(typeOf(r), t))
  }

  axiom nothingEmpty {
    (forall r: Ref ::!isSubtype(typeOf(r), nothingType()))
  }

  axiom nullSmartcastTypeLevel {
    (forall t1: rt, t2: rt ::
      { isSubtype(t1, anyType()), isSubtype(t1, nullable(t2)) }
      isSubtype(t1, anyType()) && isSubtype(t1, nullable(t2)) ==>
      isSubtype(t1, t2))
  }

  axiom typeOfNull {
    isSubtype(typeOf(nullValue()), nullable(nothingType()))
  }

  axiom anyNotNullableValueLevel {
    !isSubtype(typeOf(nullValue()), anyType())
  }

  axiom typeOfUnit {
    isSubtype(typeOf(unitValue()), unitType())
  }

  axiom uniquenessOfUnit {
    (forall r: Ref ::
      { isSubtype(typeOf(r), unitType()) }
      isSubtype(typeOf(r), unitType()) ==> r == unitValue())
  }

  axiom {
    (forall r: Ref ::
      { intFromRef(r) }
      isSubtype(typeOf(r), intType()) ==>
      intFromRef(r) >= -2147483648 && intFromRef(r) <= 2147483647)
  }

  axiom {
    (forall v: Int ::
      { isSubtype(typeOf(intToRef(v)), intType()) }
      v >= -2147483648 && v <= 2147483647 ==>
      isSubtype(typeOf(intToRef(v)), intType()))
  }

  axiom {
    (forall v: Int ::
      { intFromRef(intToRef(v)) }
      intFromRef(intToRef(v)) == v)
  }

  axiom {
    (forall r: Ref ::
      { intFromRef(r) }
      isSubtype(typeOf(r), intType()) ==> intToRef(intFromRef(r)) == r)
  }

  axiom {
    (forall r: Ref ::
      { longFromRef(r) }
      isSubtype(typeOf(r), longType()) ==>
      longFromRef(r) >= -9223372036854775808 &&
      longFromRef(r) <= 9223372036854775807)
  }

  axiom {
    (forall v: Int ::
      { isSubtype(typeOf(longToRef(v)), longType()) }
      v >= -9223372036854775808 && v <= 9223372036854775807 ==>
      isSubtype(typeOf(longToRef(v)), longType()))
  }

  axiom {
    (forall v: Int ::
      { longFromRef(longToRef(v)) }
      longFromRef(longToRef(v)) == v)
  }

  axiom {
    (forall r: Ref ::
      { longFromRef(r) }
      isSubtype(typeOf(r), longType()) ==> longToRef(longFromRef(r)) == r)
  }

  axiom {
    (forall r: Ref ::
      { shortFromRef(r) }
      isSubtype(typeOf(r), shortType()) ==>
      shortFromRef(r) >= -32768 && shortFromRef(r) <= 32767)
  }

  axiom {
    (forall v: Int ::
      { isSubtype(typeOf(shortToRef(v)), shortType()) }
      v >= -32768 && v <= 32767 ==>
      isSubtype(typeOf(shortToRef(v)), shortType()))
  }

  axiom {
    (forall v: Int ::
      { shortFromRef(shortToRef(v)) }
      shortFromRef(shortToRef(v)) == v)
  }

  axiom {
    (forall r: Ref ::
      { shortFromRef(r) }
      isSubtype(typeOf(r), shortType()) ==>
      shortToRef(shortFromRef(r)) == r)
  }

  axiom {
    (forall r: Ref ::
      { byteFromRef(r) }
      isSubtype(typeOf(r), byteType()) ==>
      byteFromRef(r) >= -128 && byteFromRef(r) <= 127)
  }

  axiom {
    (forall v: Int ::
      { isSubtype(typeOf(byteToRef(v)), byteType()) }
      v >= -128 && v <= 127 ==> isSubtype(typeOf(byteToRef(v)), byteType()))
  }

  axiom {
    (forall v: Int ::
      { byteFromRef(byteToRef(v)) }
      byteFromRef(byteToRef(v)) == v)
  }

  axiom {
    (forall r: Ref ::
      { byteFromRef(r) }
      isSubtype(typeOf(r), byteType()) ==> byteToRef(byteFromRef(r)) == r)
  }

  axiom {
    (forall v: Bool ::
      { isSubtype(typeOf(boolToRef(v)), boolType()) }
      isSubtype(typeOf(boolToRef(v)), boolType()))
  }

  axiom {
    (forall v: Bool ::
      { boolFromRef(boolToRef(v)) }
      boolFromRef(boolToRef(v)) == v)
  }

  axiom {
    (forall r: Ref ::
      { boolFromRef(r) }
      isSubtype(typeOf(r), boolType()) ==> boolToRef(boolFromRef(r)) == r)
  }

  axiom {
    (forall r: Ref ::
      { charFromRef(r) }
      isSubtype(typeOf(r), charType()) ==>
      charFromRef(r) >= 0 && charFromRef(r) <= 65535)
  }

  axiom {
    (forall v: Int ::
      { isSubtype(typeOf(charToRef(v)), charType()) }
      v >= 0 && v <= 65535 ==> isSubtype(typeOf(charToRef(v)), charType()))
  }

  axiom {
    (forall v: Int ::
      { charFromRef(charToRef(v)) }
      charFromRef(charToRef(v)) == v)
  }

  axiom {
    (forall r: Ref ::
      { charFromRef(r) }
      isSubtype(typeOf(r), charType()) ==> charToRef(charFromRef(r)) == r)
  }

  axiom {
    (forall r: Ref ::
      { stringFromRef(r) }
      isSubtype(typeOf(r), stringType()) ==>
      |stringFromRef(r)| <= 2147483647 &&
      (forall index: Int ::
        { stringFromRef(r)[index] }
        index >= 0 && index < |stringFromRef(r)| ==>
        stringFromRef(r)[index] >= 0 && stringFromRef(r)[index] <= 65535))
  }

  axiom {
    (forall v: Seq[Int] ::
      { isSubtype(typeOf(stringToRef(v)), stringType()) }
      |v| <= 2147483647 &&
      (forall index: Int ::
        { v[index] }
        index >= 0 && index < |v| ==> v[index] >= 0 && v[index] <= 65535) ==>
      isSubtype(typeOf(stringToRef(v)), stringType()))
  }

  axiom {
    (forall v: Seq[Int] ::
      { stringFromRef(stringToRef(v)) }
      stringFromRef(stringToRef(v)) == v)
  }

  axiom {
    (forall r: Ref ::
      { stringFromRef(r) }
      isSubtype(typeOf(r), stringType()) ==>
      stringToRef(stringFromRef(r)) == r)
  }
}

function addCharInt(arg_1: Ref, arg_2: Ref): Ref
  requires isSubtype(typeOf(arg_1), charType())
  requires isSubtype(typeOf(arg_2), intType())
  requires charFromRef(arg_1) + intFromRef(arg_2) >= 0 &&
    charFromRef(arg_1) + intFromRef(arg_2) <= 65535
  ensures isSubtype(typeOf(result), charType())
  ensures charFromRef(result) == charFromRef(arg_1) + intFromRef(arg_2)


function addStringChar(arg_1: Ref, arg_2: Ref): Ref
  requires isSubtype(typeOf(arg_1), stringType())
  requires isSubtype(typeOf(arg_2), charType())
  ensures isSubtype(typeOf(result), stringType())
  ensures |stringFromRef(arg_1)| < 2147483647 ==>
    stringFromRef(result) ==
    stringFromRef(arg_1) ++ Seq(charFromRef(arg_2))


function addStrings(arg_1: Ref, arg_2: Ref): Ref
  requires isSubtype(typeOf(arg_1), stringType())
  requires isSubtype(typeOf(arg_2), stringType())
  ensures isSubtype(typeOf(result), stringType())
  ensures |stringFromRef(arg_1)| + |stringFromRef(arg_2)| <= 2147483647 ==>
    stringFromRef(result) == stringFromRef(arg_1) ++ stringFromRef(arg_2)


function andBools(arg_1: Ref, arg_2: Ref): Ref
  ensures isSubtype(typeOf(result), boolType())
  ensures boolFromRef(result) == (boolFromRef(arg_1) && boolFromRef(arg_2))


function charCode(arg_1: Ref): Ref
  requires isSubtype(typeOf(arg_1), charType())
  ensures isSubtype(typeOf(result), intType())
  ensures intFromRef(result) == charFromRef(arg_1)


function compareChars(arg_1: Ref, arg_2: Ref): Ref
  requires isSubtype(typeOf(arg_1), charType())
  requires isSubtype(typeOf(arg_2), charType())
  ensures isSubtype(typeOf(result), intType())
  ensures intFromRef(result) ==
    (charFromRef(arg_1) < charFromRef(arg_2) ?
      -1 :
      (charFromRef(arg_1) > charFromRef(arg_2) ? 1 : 0))

function decByte(arg_1: Ref): Ref
  requires isSubtype(typeOf(arg_1), byteType())
  requires byteFromRef(arg_1) - 1 >= -128 && byteFromRef(arg_1) - 1 <= 127
  ensures isSubtype(typeOf(result), byteType())

        shortFromRef(arg_2))) *
    shortFromRef(arg_2) >=
    -9223372036854775808 &&
    longFromRef(arg_1) -
    (longFromRef(arg_1) < 0 != shortFromRef(arg_2) < 0 ?
      -((longFromRef(arg_1) < 0 ? -longFromRef(arg_1) : longFromRef(arg_1)) \

        -shortFromRef(arg_2) :
        shortFromRef(arg_2))) :

      (shortFromRef(arg_2) < 0 ?
        -shortFromRef(arg_2) :
        shortFromRef(arg_2))) *
    shortFromRef(arg_2) <=
    9223372036854775807
  ensures isSubtype(typeOf(result), longType())
  ensures longFromRef(result) ==
    longFromRef(arg_1) -
    (longFromRef(arg_1) < 0 != shortFromRef(arg_2) < 0 ?
      -((longFromRef(arg_1) < 0 ? -longFromRef(arg_1) : longFromRef(arg_1)) \

        -shortFromRef(arg_2) :

  requires isSubtype(typeOf(arg_1), stringType())

    intFromRef(arg_2) < |stringFromRef(arg_1)|

  ensures charFromRef(result) == stringFromRef(arg_1)[intFromRef(arg_2)]

predicate A_unique(this: Ref) {
  isSubtype(typeOf(this), A()) && acc(this.bf_unit, write) &&
  isSubtype(typeOf(this.bf_unit), unitType()) &&
  acc(this.bf_nothing, write) &&
  isSubtype(typeOf(this.bf_nothing), nothingType()) &&
  acc(this.bf_any, write) &&
  isSubtype(typeOf(this.bf_any), anyType()) &&
  acc(this.bf_int, write) &&
  isSubtype(typeOf(this.bf_int), intType()) &&
  acc(this.bf_boolean, write) &&
  isSubtype(typeOf(this.bf_boolean), boolType()) &&
  acc(this.bf_char, write) &&
  isSubtype(typeOf(this.bf_char), charType()) &&
  acc(this.bf_string, write) &&
  isSubtype(typeOf(this.bf_string), stringType()) &&
  acc(this.bf_classType, write) &&
  isSubtype(typeOf(this.bf_classType), B()) &&
  acc(this.bf_unitNull, write) &&
  isSubtype(typeOf(this.bf_unitNull), nullable(unitType())) &&
  acc(this.bf_nothingNull, write) &&
  isSubtype(typeOf(this.bf_nothingNull), nullable(nothingType())) &&
  acc(this.bf_anyNull, write) &&
  isSubtype(typeOf(this.bf_anyNull), nullable(anyType())) &&
  acc(this.bf_intNull, write) &&
  isSubtype(typeOf(this.bf_intNull), nullable(intType())) &&
  acc(this.bf_booleanNull, write) &&
  isSubtype(typeOf(this.bf_booleanNull), nullable(boolType())) &&
  acc(this.bf_charNull, write) &&
  isSubtype(typeOf(this.bf_charNull), nullable(charType())) &&
  acc(this.bf_stringNull, write) &&
  isSubtype(typeOf(this.bf_stringNull), nullable(stringType())) &&
  acc(this.bf_classTypeNull, write) &&
  isSubtype(typeOf(this.bf_classTypeNull), nullable(B()))
}

predicate B_unique(this: Ref) {
  isSubtype(typeOf(this), B())
}

method con(par_unit: Ref, par_nothing: Ref, par_any: Ref, par_int: Ref, par_boolean: Ref,
  par_char: Ref, par_string: Ref, par_classType: Ref, par_unitNull: Ref, par_nothingNull: Ref,
  par_anyNull: Ref, par_intNull: Ref, par_booleanNull: Ref, par_charNull: Ref,
  par_stringNull: Ref, par_classTypeNull: Ref)
  returns (v_ret_0: Ref)
  requires isSubtype(typeOf(par_unit), unitType())
  requires false
  requires isSubtype(typeOf(par_nothing), nothingType())
  requires isSubtype(typeOf(par_any), anyType())
  requires isSubtype(typeOf(par_int), intType())
  requires isSubtype(typeOf(par_boolean), boolType())
  requires isSubtype(typeOf(par_char), charType())
  requires isSubtype(typeOf(par_string), stringType())
  requires isSubtype(typeOf(par_classType), B())
  requires isSubtype(typeOf(par_unitNull), nullable(unitType()))
  requires par_nothingNull != nullValue() ==> false
  requires isSubtype(typeOf(par_nothingNull), nullable(nothingType()))
  requires isSubtype(typeOf(par_anyNull), nullable(anyType()))
  requires isSubtype(typeOf(par_intNull), nullable(intType()))
  requires isSubtype(typeOf(par_booleanNull), nullable(boolType()))
  requires isSubtype(typeOf(par_charNull), nullable(charType()))
  requires isSubtype(typeOf(par_stringNull), nullable(stringType()))
  requires isSubtype(typeOf(par_classTypeNull), nullable(B()))
  ensures false
  ensures par_nothingNull != nullValue() ==> false

  axiom subtypeReflexive {
    (forall t: rt ::isSubtype(t, t))
  }

  axiom subtypeTransitive {
    (forall t1: rt, t2: rt, t3: rt ::
      { isSubtype(t1, t2), isSubtype(t2, t3) }
      { isSubtype(t1, t2), isSubtype(t1, t3) }
      { isSubtype(t2, t3), isSubtype(t1, t3) }
      isSubtype(t1, t2) && isSubtype(t2, t3) ==> isSubtype(t1, t3))
  }

function divByteByte(arg_1: Ref, arg_2: Ref): Ref
  requires isSubtype(typeOf(arg_1), byteType())
  requires isSubtype(typeOf(arg_2), byteType())
  requires byteFromRef(arg_2) != 0
  requires (byteFromRef(arg_1) < 0 != byteFromRef(arg_2) < 0 ?
      -((byteFromRef(arg_1) < 0 ? -byteFromRef(arg_1) : byteFromRef(arg_1)) \
      (byteFromRef(arg_2) < 0 ? -byteFromRef(arg_2) : byteFromRef(arg_2))) :
      (byteFromRef(arg_1) < 0 ? -byteFromRef(arg_1) : byteFromRef(arg_1)) \
      (byteFromRef(arg_2) < 0 ? -byteFromRef(arg_2) : byteFromRef(arg_2))) >=
    -2147483648 &&
    (byteFromRef(arg_1) < 0 != byteFromRef(arg_2) < 0 ?
      -((byteFromRef(arg_1) < 0 ? -byteFromRef(arg_1) : byteFromRef(arg_1)) \
      (byteFromRef(arg_2) < 0 ? -byteFromRef(arg_2) : byteFromRef(arg_2))) :
      (byteFromRef(arg_1) < 0 ? -byteFromRef(arg_1) : byteFromRef(arg_1)) \
      (byteFromRef(arg_2) < 0 ? -byteFromRef(arg_2) : byteFromRef(arg_2))) <=
    2147483647
  ensures isSubtype(typeOf(result), intType())
  ensures intFromRef(result) ==
    (byteFromRef(arg_1) < 0 != byteFromRef(arg_2) < 0 ?
      -((byteFromRef(arg_1) < 0 ? -byteFromRef(arg_1) : byteFromRef(arg_1)) \
      (byteFromRef(arg_2) < 0 ? -byteFromRef(arg_2) : byteFromRef(arg_2))) :
      (byteFromRef(arg_1) < 0 ? -byteFromRef(arg_1) : byteFromRef(arg_1)) \
      (byteFromRef(arg_2) < 0 ? -byteFromRef(arg_2) : byteFromRef(arg_2)))

  requires isSubtype(typeOf(arg_1), byteType())

    (byteFromRef(arg_1) < 0 != byteFromRef(arg_2) < 0 ?
      -((byteFromRef(arg_1) < 0 ? -byteFromRef(arg_1) : byteFromRef(arg_1)) \

      (byteFromRef(arg_2) < 0 ? -byteFromRef(arg_2) : byteFromRef(arg_2))) *
    byteFromRef(arg_2) >=
    -2147483648 &&
    byteFromRef(arg_1) -
    (byteFromRef(arg_1) < 0 != byteFromRef(arg_2) < 0 ?
      -((byteFromRef(arg_1) < 0 ? -byteFromRef(arg_1) : byteFromRef(arg_1)) \
      (byteFromRef(arg_2) < 0 ? -byteFromRef(arg_2) : byteFromRef(arg_2))) :
      (byteFromRef(arg_1) < 0 ? -byteFromRef(arg_1) : byteFromRef(arg_1)) \
      (byteFromRef(arg_2) < 0 ? -byteFromRef(arg_2) : byteFromRef(arg_2))) *
    byteFromRef(arg_2) <=
    2147483647
  ensures isSubtype(typeOf(result), intType())
  ensures intFromRef(result) ==
    byteFromRef(arg_1) -
    (byteFromRef(arg_1) < 0 != byteFromRef(arg_2) < 0 ?
      -((byteFromRef(arg_1) < 0 ? -byteFromRef(arg_1) : byteFromRef(arg_1)) \
      (byteFromRef(arg_2) < 0 ? -byteFromRef(arg_2) : byteFromRef(arg_2))) :

      { isSubtype(t1, t2), isSubtype(t1, t3) }

function divByteByte(arg_1: Ref, arg_2: Ref): Ref
  requires isSubtype(typeOf(arg_1), byteType())
  requires isSubtype(typeOf(arg_2), byteType())
  requires byteFromRef(arg_2) != 0
  requires (byteFromRef(arg_1) < 0 != byteFromRef(arg_2) < 0 ?
      -((byteFromRef(arg_1) < 0 ? -byteFromRef(arg_1) : byteFromRef(arg_1)) \
      (byteFromRef(arg_2) < 0 ? -byteFromRef(arg_2) : byteFromRef(arg_2))) :
      (byteFromRef(arg_1) < 0 ? -byteFromRef(arg_1) : byteFromRef(arg_1)) \
      (byteFromRef(arg_2) < 0 ? -byteFromRef(arg_2) : byteFromRef(arg_2))) >=
    -2147483648 &&
    (byteFromRef(arg_1) < 0 != byteFromRef(arg_2) < 0 ?
      -((byteFromRef(arg_1) < 0 ? -byteFromRef(arg_1) : byteFromRef(arg_1)) \
      (byteFromRef(arg_2) < 0 ? -byteFromRef(arg_2) : byteFromRef(arg_2))) :
      (byteFromRef(arg_1) < 0 ? -byteFromRef(arg_1) : byteFromRef(arg_1)) \
      (byteFromRef(arg_2) < 0 ? -byteFromRef(arg_2) : byteFromRef(arg_2))) <=
    2147483647
  ensures isSubtype(typeOf(result), intType())
  ensures intFromRef(result) ==
    (byteFromRef(arg_1) < 0 != byteFromRef(arg_2) < 0 ?
      -((byteFromRef(arg_1) < 0 ? -byteFromRef(arg_1) : byteFromRef(arg_1)) \
      (byteFromRef(arg_2) < 0 ? -byteFromRef(arg_2) : byteFromRef(arg_2))) :
      (byteFromRef(arg_1) < 0 ? -byteFromRef(arg_1) : byteFromRef(arg_1)) \
      (byteFromRef(arg_2) < 0 ? -byteFromRef(arg_2) : byteFromRef(arg_2)))

function remByteByte(arg_1: Ref, arg_2: Ref): Ref
  requires isSubtype(typeOf(arg_1), byteType())
  requires isSubtype(typeOf(arg_2), byteType())
  requires byteFromRef(arg_2) != 0
  requires byteFromRef(arg_1) -
    (byteFromRef(arg_1) < 0 != byteFromRef(arg_2) < 0 ?
      -((byteFromRef(arg_1) < 0 ? -byteFromRef(arg_1) : byteFromRef(arg_1)) \
      (byteFromRef(arg_2) < 0 ? -byteFromRef(arg_2) : byteFromRef(arg_2))) :
      (byteFromRef(arg_1) < 0 ? -byteFromRef(arg_1) : byteFromRef(arg_1)) \
      (byteFromRef(arg_2) < 0 ? -byteFromRef(arg_2) : byteFromRef(arg_2))) *
    byteFromRef(arg_2) >=
    -2147483648 &&
    byteFromRef(arg_1) -
    (byteFromRef(arg_1) < 0 != byteFromRef(arg_2) < 0 ?
      -((byteFromRef(arg_1) < 0 ? -byteFromRef(arg_1) : byteFromRef(arg_1)) \
      (byteFromRef(arg_2) < 0 ? -byteFromRef(arg_2) : byteFromRef(arg_2))) :
      (byteFromRef(arg_1) < 0 ? -byteFromRef(arg_1) : byteFromRef(arg_1)) \
      (byteFromRef(arg_2) < 0 ? -byteFromRef(arg_2) : byteFromRef(arg_2))) *
    byteFromRef(arg_2) <=
    2147483647
  ensures isSubtype(typeOf(result), intType())
  ensures intFromRef(result) ==
    byteFromRef(arg_1) -
    (byteFromRef(arg_1) < 0 != byteFromRef(arg_2) < 0 ?
      -((byteFromRef(arg_1) < 0 ? -byteFromRef(arg_1) : byteFromRef(arg_1)) \
      (byteFromRef(arg_2) < 0 ? -byteFromRef(arg_2) : byteFromRef(arg_2))) :
      (byteFromRef(arg_1) < 0 ? -byteFromRef(arg_1) : byteFromRef(arg_1)) \
      (byteFromRef(arg_2) < 0 ? -byteFromRef(arg_2) : byteFromRef(arg_2))) *
    byteFromRef(arg_2)

  requires longFromRef(arg_1) * byteFromRef(arg_2) >= -9223372036854775808 &&
    longFromRef(arg_1) * byteFromRef(arg_2) <= 9223372036854775807
  ensures isSubtype(typeOf(result), longType())
  ensures longFromRef(result) == longFromRef(arg_1) * byteFromRef(arg_2)

function xorBools(arg_1: Ref, arg_2: Ref): Ref
  ensures isSubtype(typeOf(result), boolType())
  ensures boolFromRef(result) == (boolFromRef(arg_1) != boolFromRef(arg_2))


predicate A_unique(this: Ref) {
  isSubtype(typeOf(this), A()) && acc(this.unit, write) &&
  isSubtype(typeOf(this.unit), unitType()) &&
  acc(this.nothing, write) &&
  isSubtype(typeOf(this.nothing), nothingType()) &&
  acc(this.any, write) &&
  isSubtype(typeOf(this.any), anyType()) &&
  acc(this.int, write) &&
  isSubtype(typeOf(this.int), intType()) &&
  acc(this.boolean, write) &&
  isSubtype(typeOf(this.boolean), boolType()) &&
  acc(this.char, write) &&
  isSubtype(typeOf(this.char), charType()) &&
  acc(this.string, write) &&
  isSubtype(typeOf(this.string), stringType()) &&
  acc(this.classType, write) &&
  isSubtype(typeOf(this.classType), B()) &&
  acc(this.unitNull, write) &&
  isSubtype(typeOf(this.unitNull), nullable(unitType())) &&
  acc(this.nothingNull, write) &&
  isSubtype(typeOf(this.nothingNull), nullable(nothingType())) &&
  acc(this.anyNull, write) &&
  isSubtype(typeOf(this.anyNull), nullable(anyType())) &&
  acc(this.intNull, write) &&
  isSubtype(typeOf(this.intNull), nullable(intType())) &&
  acc(this.booleanNull, write) &&
  isSubtype(typeOf(this.booleanNull), nullable(boolType())) &&
  acc(this.charNull, write) &&
  isSubtype(typeOf(this.charNull), nullable(charType())) &&
  acc(this.stringNull, write) &&
  isSubtype(typeOf(this.stringNull), nullable(stringType())) &&
  acc(this.classTypeNull, write) &&
  isSubtype(typeOf(this.classTypeNull), nullable(B()))
}

predicate B_unique(this: Ref) {
  isSubtype(typeOf(this), B())
}

method havoc(arg_1: rt) returns (v_ret_0: Ref)
  ensures isSubtype(typeOf(v_ret_0), arg_1)


method havocNothing(a: Ref) returns (v_ret_0: Ref)
  requires isSubtype(typeOf(a), A())
  ensures isSubtype(typeOf(v_ret_0), unitType())
{
  var localNothing: Ref
  localNothing := havoc(nothingType())
  label lbl_ret_0
  inhale isSubtype(typeOf(v_ret_0), unitType())
}
